Intercultural communication skill 
Teaching suggestions 
Step 1: Guide Ss to read through the IC skill explanation. Draw their attention to the 
strategy of “breaking down complex concepts.” Encourage them to brainstorm 
additional ways a complex concept can be simplified beyond subtopics and stages. For 
example, consider breaking it down into categories or key elements.  
校园资料仓

9 
 
 
Step 2: Lead Ss to discuss the features of “simple language.” Guide them to identify 
more examples of simplified language from the video. For example, in the sentence 
“China became the largest trading country and it contributed to over 30 percent of the 
world’s total economic growth,” rather than presenting the complex total value of 
China’s foreign trade, the video uses a simple percentage to highlight China’s global 
influence.  
Step 3: Guide Ss to discuss other possible strategies for simplification, like analogies 
and metaphors, visual aids, stories, and examples. 
Step 4: Divide the class into groups and guide them to discuss how to present the 
concepts of “Special economic zone” and “Made in China” in a simpler way. Groups 
take turns to share the results of their discussion with the whole class. Guide Ss to apply 
the discussed strategies in their explanation of these concepts. 
* Remind Ss to access the video micro-lecture in the digital textbook to learn more 
about the skill on Ucampus. 
 
Sample answers 
3 
Special economic zone: A special economic zone is a special area within a 
country where different economic rules are applied. It is a “test area” for new economic 
policies to attract investments and encourage business and trade. A properly planned 
and well-managed special economic zone can bring economic transformation and spur 
technological advancement. For example, Shenzhen was set as a special economic zone 
in China in 1980. Special policies to experiment with market-oriented reforms helped 
Shenzhen transform from a small fishing village into a global technology hub in just a 
few decades.  
(Note: The sample simplifies the concept of “a special economic zone” by 
answering two questions: “What is it?” “Why does it matter?”, and using simple 
language so that those without much background knowledge can understand the 
complicated concept.) 
Made in China: “Made in China” is a label for products manufactured in China.   
Over the years, this label has undergone a significant transformation, shifting its focus 
from low-cost goods to affordable high-tech and high-quality products like drones, 
electric vehicles, and robot products.  
(Note: In cross-cultural communication, “Made in China” is much more than a 
label of product origin. The expression reflects the economic transformation undergone 
in China and the benefits Chinese products have brought to the whole world. To explain 
the complicated implications of this expression in a simpler way, we can break the 
concept into two main phases: first, as a label for low-cost goods, and then, as a symbol 
of high-quality, innovative products.) 
校园资料仓

10 
 
 
Reading  
Text: Breezes of change from Hainan 
Background information 
In 2013, the Third Plenary Session of the 18th CPC Central Committee marked 
China’s entry into a new era of comprehensive reform and opening up. Reform efforts 
expanded in breadth and depth, achieving historic successes and shifting from localized 
trials to systematic, holistic restructuring. In 2022, the 20th National Congress of the 
CPC called for “further deepening reform comprehensively to advance Chinese 
modernization,” aiming to improve and develop the socialist system with Chinese 
characteristics, modernize the national governance system and capacity, and advance 
the building of a great nation and the rejuvenation of the Chinese nation through 
Chinese modernization. 
Facing complex domestic and international dynamics, a new wave of 
technological and industrial change, and rising public expectations, China formulated a 
strategic plan for reform grounded in the broader national and global context. It clarified 
the guiding principles, overall goals, and major reform measures, launching over 300 
key initiatives. As a result, foundational institutional frameworks have been largely 
established, many sectors have undergone systemic transformation, and the 
modernization of governance has been significantly advanced. Meanwhile, China has 
adopted a more proactive opening-up strategy, building a global network of high-
standard free trade zones, accelerating the development of pilot free trade zones and the 
Hainan Free Trade Port, and promoting the Belt and Road Initiative, strengthening its 
position as a major trading partner to over 150 countries and regions and fostering a 
broader, wider, and deeper pattern of openness. 
 
Notes 
1 
On June 1, 2020, the Chinese government released a master plan for constructing 
the Hannan Free Trade Port, setting a roadmap to building a high-level free trade 
port by 2035 and full completion by 2050. Preferential policies like tax exemptions 
and streamlined customs procedures were introduced in the island province to 
create a business-friendly environment. Over the past six years, under the 
leadership of the CPC Central Committee, the Hainan Free Trade Port (FTP) 
has become a new frontier for China’s institutional opening up, a new hub for 
regional mutually beneficial cooperation, and a new engine driving economic 
globalization. 
2 
The Yangpu Port in Danzhou, Hainan is a deep-water wind-sheltered port ideal for 
large vessels. Established in 2007, the Yangpu Bonded Port Area took the lead 
in 2020 in the implementation of an entry and exit management system for goods 
to facilitate trade.  
校园资料仓

11 
 
 
3 
Lecheng International Medical Tourism Pilot Zone is located in Boao Town, 
Qionghai City, Hainan Province, close to the core area of the Boao Forum for Asia. 
Established in 2013, this is the only “healthcare special region” in China and it 
enjoys preferential policies for specially licensed healthcare, business, research, 
international exchanges, and other services to provide medical services not 
available elsewhere in the country. As a pilot zone of Hainan FTP, Lecheng is also 
developing international medical tourism and high-end healthcare services, and has 
become a testing ground for major national projects of innovation in new drugs 
and healthcare technologies to help improve China’s healthcare system. 
4 
Starting on February 9, 2024, China expanded visa-free entry to Hainan for 
ordinary passport holders from 59 countries. Eligible travelers can stay up to 30 
days for purposes including tourism, business, family visits, medical treatment, 
exhibitions, and sports competitions (excluding work and study), entering through 
any international port in Hainan. 
5 
As part of the Yazhou Bay Science and Technology City in Sanya, Hainan, the 
Nanfan Scientific and Research Breeding Base is a critical research and breeding 
center for crop varieties and seed industry innovation in China. According to the 
Construction Plan for Building the National Nanfan Silicon Valley 2023-2030, the 
Nanfan Breeding Base in Hainan is set to evolve into the “Silicon Valley” of 
China’s seed industry by 2030, serving as a comprehensive hub for agricultural 
research, industry, and technology exchange.

Language points  
1 Spring in Hainan is a season of lush greenery and vibrant activity, where the 
gentle breeze carries the promise of transformation. (Para. 1) 
Note: The text opens with this vivid description of the spring scene in Hainan. Phrases 
like “lush greenery” and “gentle breeze” evoke visual and tactile sensations, drawing 
the reader into the season’s beauty and vitality to enhance their connection with and 
understanding of the text. 
2 The FTP is not just a special economic zone; it is a dynamic hub where goods 
can be imported, processed, and re-exported under specific customs 
regulations with low or zero taxes, representing the highest level of openness 
in today’s world. (Para. 2) 
Meaning: The FTP is more than a special economic zone – it serves as a vibrant center 
where goods can be brought in, manufactured, and shipped out with little or no taxes, 
making it one of the most open trade systems in the world today. 
校园资料仓

12 
 
 
Note: This structure “… is not just …; it is …” is often used to emphasize that 
something is more significant or complex than it might initially seem. The first part “is 
not just” sets up a limited or superficial understanding, while the second part “it is” 
introduces a deeper or more important meaning, highlighting the true significance or 
complexity of the subject. For example: 
This painting is not just a piece of art; it is a reflection of the artist’s struggle and 
passion. 
3 
Even in the dead of night, the Port of Yangpu is still bustling … (Para 3.) 
in the dead of night: in the middle of  night  在深夜 
The phone rang in the dead of night, bringing the good news we had been waiting for. 
Note: Two contrasting scenes are described in this sentence – the dead of night and the 
bustling port. This contrast helps to grab the reader’s attention and emphasize the port’s 
non-stop operation. 
4 
As a pilot demonstration zone for the Hainan FTP, the Yangpu Bonded Port 
Area is stress-testing preferential FTP policies to prepare the whole island for 
independent customs operations by 2025. (Para. 4) 
Meaning: The Yangpu Bonded Port Area is a testing ground for the preferential 
policies of the Hainan FTP. Through the implementation and evaluation of these 
policies in Yangpu, the initiative aims to prepare the entire island for the establishment 
of an independent customs system by 2025. 
stress-test:  
vt. evaluate the performance or resilience of a system, process, or entity under extreme 
or simulated conditions (进行)压力测试 
The engineers stress-tested the new software to ensure it could handle high traffic 
during peak usage times. 
Without the hyphen, “stress test” is a noun, referring to “a method to evaluate the 
performance or resilience of a system, process, or entity under extreme or simulated 
conditions(压力测试).” For example: 
Before launching the new app, the development team ran a stress test to ensure it could 
handle millions of users simultaneously without crashing. 
5 
In the year 2024, its import and export value reached RMB66.57 billion, an 8 
percent year-on-year increase. (Para. 5) 
year-on-year: a. comparing or based on comparing the same time period in successive 
years 同比的；较去年同期相比的.  
The company reported a 5 percent year-on-year increase in sales. 
Note: When the hyphens are removed, “year on year” functions as an adverb. For 
example, the sentence above can be rewritten as: The company reported a 5 percent 
increase in sales year on year. 
校园资料仓

13 
 
 
6 
As an international medical tourism pilot zone, the city is in sync with 
advanced international medical technology, equipment, and pharmaceuticals, 
enabling the girl to embrace the gift of sound at the optimal time. (Para. 6) 
in sync with: 
1) working together at the same time, at the same speed, or in the same way 与…同
步的 
The robots move in sync with the beat of the music. 
2) matching or in agreement 与…协调的；一致的 
Her actions were completely in sync with her values. 
7 
With a new policy for visa-free entry for citizens of 59 countries in play in 
Hainan since early 2024, Lecheng’s magnetism is only growing. (Para. 8) 
in play: in effect; in action 实施；活动中 
With all the new regulations in play, businesses had to adapt quickly to remain 
competitive. 
8 
Through these ventures, Lecheng is on the fast track to becoming a premier 
global destination for medical tourism and a nexus for medical innovation, 
effectively catering to the health needs of both locals and international 
visitors. (Para. 9) 
on the fast track: in a position on the quickest route to a successful position 在通往成
功的快车道上 
The new employee was put on the fast track to a leadership position due to her 
exceptional performance and innovative ideas. 
nexus: n. [sing.] (fml.)  
1) the central and most important point of place 中心；交叉点；汇聚焦点 
The bookstore has become something of a nexus for the downtown neighborhood. 
2) a connection or network of connections between a number of people, things, or 
ideas （人﹑事物或思想之间的）关系，关联，联系，联结 
The nexus between education and economic growth is well-documented. 
9 
Abundant sunlight and a winterless climate all year round make Hainan a 
paradise for seed breeding research. (Para. 10) 
Meaning: The plentiful sunlight and warm climate in Hainan create an ideal 
environment for seed breeding research, as they allow for faster crop growth cycles and 
uninterrupted agricultural experimentation. 
10 China is doubling down on its commitment to opening up to the world, 
pushing forward development and contributing to a global narrative of an 
open world economy. (Para. 12) 
double down (on sth.): (usu. AmE) continue to do sth. in an even more determined way 
than before 加倍努力；加大力度；更加坚定地坚持

Translation  
来自海南的变革之风 
1 
海南的春天，绿意盎然、生机勃勃，和煦的微风中蕴藏着变革的气息。这
座美丽的岛屿现今已崛起为中国通向世界的重要门户。随着海南自由贸易
港（FTP）的发展，这座岛屿已立于中国新一轮改革开放的最前沿，成为连
接东北亚和东南亚的重要纽带。 
2 
海南自由贸易港不仅是一个经济特区，更是一个充满活力的枢纽。在这
里，货物可依特定海关监管政策以低关税甚至零税率进口、加工、再出
口，体现了当今世界最高水平的开放性。随着自贸港建设的不断推进，海
南的每一天都在书写新的篇章。 
凌晨1:00，洋浦保税港区 
3 
即使在深夜，洋浦港依然繁忙——巨轮靠港、塔吊挥舞、五颜六色的集装
箱装卸作业，码头上卡车往来穿梭，许多人昼夜不停地忙碌。“以前办理
清关提货需要一到两天，现在有了简化申报政策，仅需半小时，通关效率
提高了不少。”一位市场经理介绍道。 
4 
作为海南自贸港的试验示范区，洋浦保税港区正在进行压力测试，为全岛
在2025 年实现独立海关运作做好准备。除了简化申报程序，这里还施行了
免关税和低税率等优惠政策。 
5 
新政策的实施让洋浦保税港区一片繁荣。截至目前，这个不到2.3 平方公里
的区域已吸引了5,834 家企业。2024 年该区域的进出口总额达665.7 亿元人
民币，同比增长8%。所有这些都将助力海南自贸港在本世纪中叶成为具有
重要国际影响力的高水平自由贸易港。 
上午10:00，乐城国际医疗旅游先行区 
6 
一名四岁的小女孩正在乐城的一家医院接受手术，植入来自奥地利的最新
电子助听设备。作为国际医疗旅游先行区，乐城与国际先进的医疗技术、
设备和药品接轨，让这名女孩在最佳的年龄段接受移植，感受到声音的美
妙。 
7 
乐城，这座罕见和疑难病患者的希望之城，正在进一步改革医疗审批程
序。截至2024 年底，450 多种进口药品和医疗设备获批在这里使用，造福
10 万多名患者，为他们节省大量费用，例如在美国进行人工角膜移植手术

至少需10 万美元，而这种手术在乐城仅需1.5 万美元。 
8 
医疗技术的先进性和可及性，加上海南宜人的自然风光和气候，使乐城成

下午2:00，南繁科研育种基地 
10 充足的阳光和全年无冬的气候，使海南成为育种研究的天堂。在海南的南
繁科研育种基地，种子专家们正在观察实验田中最近播种的玉米种子——
这些种子远从巴西跨洋而来。 
11 南繁科研育种基地正随着自贸港的发展而不断进化。依托自由贸易港这一
国际化通道，南繁的科学家们有更多优质种源可选。得益于技术进步，种
子专家现在可以远程收集并分析作物整个生长周期的实时数据，加快研究
进程及其实际应用。大幅度的税收优惠和高额的补助吸引了投资，也引得
研究机构和知名种业公司纷纷入驻。到2030 年，一个集研究、生产、销
售、科技交流及成果转化为一体的“南繁硅谷”将全面建成，造福中国并
助力解决全球粮食安全问题。 
12 太阳再次升起，为海南岛蓬勃发展的景象披上金色的光芒。海南自贸港的
建设正如火如荼，开启了海南改革开放之旅的新篇章。中国正加大对外开
放力度，推动发展，积极参与全球开放型经济体系的构建。

Intercultural communication skill  
Teaching suggestions 
⚫ 
Help Ss to understand that a rich context is crucial for communication across 
cultural boundaries. Guide Ss to read through the illustrations on situation-based 
narratives in the textbook.  
⚫ 
Invite some Ss to explain the two example strategies used in the Yangpu story in 
their own words. 
⚫ 
Guide Ss to discuss other possible strategies to create a rich context, for example:  
➢ Name cities, landmarks, or regions, or describe the cultural or geographical 
significance of the location. 
➢ Describe people, objects, or events with sensory details, or use sensory details 
to evoke a sense of place. 
➢ Share personal anecdotes or experiences that evoke universal emotions. 
➢ Structure the narrative in a logical, easy-to-follow sequence. 
➢ Describe characters’ roles, motivations, and emotions. 
➢ … 
⚫ 
Divide Ss into pairs and guide them to discuss how the author of the text engages 
the audience through a situation-based narrative in the Lecheng and Nanfan stories. 
* Remind Ss to access the video micro-lecture in the digital textbook to learn more 
about the skill on Ucampus. 
 
Sample answers 
Just like the description of the Por of Yangpu, both the Lecheng and Nanfan stories 
begin with a scene at a specific time. 
Lecheng International Medical Tourism Pilot Zone: Instead of a bustling view 
with multiple elements, the Lecheng story begins with a simple narration about a four-
year-old girl undergoing a hearing device implantation surgery. The mentioning of the 
details – the girl’s age, the device from Austria, and the optimal time – fosters the 
校园资料仓

17 
 
 
readers’ empathy and helps them to understand why Lecheng is called “a city of hope.” 
Nanfan Scientific and Research Breeding Base: The text introduces the Nanfan 
Scientific and Research Breeding Base with a vivid scene of researchers observing 
seeds. Details like “the corn seeds,” “recently planted,” and “traveled all the way across 
the ocean from Brazil,” help to immerse the readers in the scene and helpthem to 
understand the effect of the “opening up.”

As a university student, I have witnessed the profound changes brought about by 
reform and opening up. With a series of policies encouraging innovation and promoting 
development now in play, society continues to radiate new vitality. Reform and opening 
up is now in full swing, creating unprecedented opportunities for young people to

Critical thinking 
Sample answers  
1 
In addition to the construction of the Hainan Free Trade Port, a series of significant 
measures have also been implemented to deepen the reform and opening up in the new 
era. These reforms focus on refining institutional frameworks and operational 
mechanisms to support innovation, fairness, and sustainable development, and to 
promote a high-standard socialist market economy system. A rural revitalization 
strategy is introduced to improve rural infrastructure, promote agricultural 
modernization, and create a more balanced economic structure. Funding is set up to 
promote scientific and technological self-reliance. Measures are taken to boost 
domestic consumption. The domestic reforms are accompanied by stronger opening-up 
initiatives, such as the construction of the Guangdong-Hong Kong-Macao Greater Bay 
Area and the high-quality development of the Belt and Road Initiative.  
These new measures differ from previous ones. First, greater emphasis is placed 
on high-quality development, focusing on economic growth that is efficient, inclusive, 
and sustainable, rather than merely on GDP growth. Second, innovation-driven 
strategies are encouraged, moving away from reliance on low-cost labor and resource-
intensive industries. The new measures leverage science, technology, and innovation as 
the primary engines of economic growth. What’s more, economic growth is achieved 
together with the minimization of environmental degradation, carbon emissions 
reduction, and the sustainable use of natural resources. 
 
2 
These measures to further advance the modernization of China’s governance 
system and capabilities in the new era reflect the new development concepts that are in 
sync with the new national and international situation. 
First, reform in the new era, as a comprehensive endeavor, covers all aspects and 
fields, including the economy, politics, culture, society, ecological civilization, national 
defense, and military reform, as well as the reform of the governance system of the 
Communist Party of China. Its overall goal is to improve and develop the system of 
socialism with Chinese characteristics and to advance the modernization of China’s 
system and capacity for governance. Under this overarching goal, specific sub-goals 
have been set for deepening the reform across various sectors, ensuring that different 
reforms are integrated into an organic whole. 
Second, reform in the new era is closely intertwined with high-level opening up, 
with the two processes reinforcing each other to achieve greater development in the 
new historical conditions. Concrete plans have been made for building a higher-
standard open economy, with an emphasis on promoting broader, wider, and deeper 
校园资料仓

19 
 
 
opening up, and advancing institutional opening up in areas such as rules, regulations, 
management, and standards. 
 
 
校园资料仓

20 
 
 
Exploring the world  
Thanks to its continuous reform efforts and opening-up policies, China continues 
to achieve sustained growth in the dynamic world. Similarly, many other countries are 
striving to forge their own paths to progress. For example, the countries in the African 
continent are making collective efforts under the AfCFTA framework to establish a 
unified market for goods, services, investment, and people across the member states. 
This initiative aims to facilitate intra-African trade, promote regional integration, drive 
economic diversification, and ultimately deliver greater growth and prosperity to all 
member countries. 
The key points focus on helping Ss understand the challenges involved in 
achieving African economic integration and the key strategies to address them, 
including reducing trade barriers and harmonizing policies. The difficult points lie in 
guiding Ss to grasp the background and significance of the AfCFTA. 
Africa’s commitment to free trade, the elimination of trade barriers, and 
multilateral cooperation aligns with the broader context of economic globalization – an 
inevitable and unstoppable trend driving global progress. These efforts can be 
compared to China’s unwavering support for economic globalization, its emphasis on 
safeguarding free trade, resolving trade disputes through dialogue and consultation, and 
promoting collective action to ensure the stability and fairness of the global trade 
system. Meanwhile, counter-globalization trends, such as the rise of protectionism and 
unilateral actions like the US trade wars, should be critically examined.  
 
Text: New horizons in African trade 
Background information 
Africa, the second-largest continent in terms of both land area and population, 
comprises over 50 recognized independent countries with significantly uneven levels 
of development, with huge gaps between high-income, middle-income, and low-
income countries. To promote unity, development, and peace across the African 
continent, the African Union (AU) was established in 2002. Inspired by the vision of 
“an integrated, prosperous, and peaceful Africa, driven by its own citizens and 
representing a dynamic force in the international arena,” the AU launched Agenda 2063 
in 2015 as a strategic framework for the socio-economic transformation of the 
continent.  
The African Continental Free Trade Area (AfCFTA), which was established in 
2018, came into force in 2019 and was officially launched in 2021, as one of the flagship 
projects of Agenda 2063. The AfCFTA aims to create a single continental market for 
goods and services, fostering regional cooperation, reducing trade barriers, and 
promoting inclusive growth. By addressing disparities through policies that encourage 
industrialization, infrastructure development, and economic diversification, the 
校园资料仓

21 
 
 
AfCFTA represents a pioneering effort by African countries to integrate their 
economies and strengthen their global competitiveness. 
 
Notes 
1 
The World Bank Group is the largest multilateral development institution in the 
world, dedicated to helping developing countries eliminate poverty and promote 
sustainable development. Headquartered in Washington, DC, the capital of the 
United States, the World Bank consists of five member organizations: the 
International Bank for Reconstruction and Development (IBRD), the International 
Development Association (IDA), the International Finance Corporation (IFC), the 
Multilateral Investment Guarantee Agency (MIGA), and the International Centre 
for Settlement of Investment Disputes (ICSID). 
2 
As the pillars of the AU, the Regional Economic Communities (RECs) are 
regional groupings of African states that were formed prior to the launch of the 
AU. The REGs aim to facilitate regional economic integration between members 
of the individual regions. The AU recognizes eight RECs: 
• Arab Maghreb Union (UMA) 
• Common Market for Eastern and Southern Africa (COMESA) 
• Community of Sahel–Saharan States (CEN–SAD) 
• East African Community (EAC) 
• Economic Community of Central African States (ECCAS) 
• Economic Community of West African States (ECOWAS) 
• Intergovernmental Authority on Development (IGAD) 
• Southern African Development Community (SADC) 
*T can guide Ss to study the multimodal annotations in the digital textbook on 
Ucampus.  
 
Language points 
1 The supermarket shelves in Freetown, Sierra Leone are bursting at the seams 
with imported goods, ranging from Chinese toothpicks and Dutch toilet paper 
to French sugar and Swiss chocolates. (Para. 1) 
be bursting at the seams: be very full of people or things 过于拥挤；过满 
The stadium was bursting at the seams as thousands of fans gathered to watch the 
championship match. 
Note: This sentence is a good example of a situation-based narrative. The detailed 
description of imported goods from various countries helps readers to visualize the 
supermarket shelves in the African city. 
校园资料仓

22 
 
 
2 So why do retailers source them from far-off countries instead of local 
producers? (Para. 1) 
source (from): vt. obtain sth. from a particular place 从…获取 
The chef prefers to source produce from local farmers to get the freshest ingredients 
for his dishes. 
Note: This rhetorical question is asked to draw the readers’ attention to the problem the 
AfCFTA tries to address. 
3 
The answer lies in a patchwork of trade regulations and tariffs that tend to 
make intra-African commerce costly, inconsistent, and cumbersome. (Para. 
1) 
Note: Here the word “patchwork” refers to a fragmented and inconsistent system of 
trade regulations and tariffs; “costly” reflects the high expenses associated with cross-
border trade; “inconsistent” indicates the lack of uniformity, stability, or predictability; 
and “cumbersome” refers to the complex and time-consuming processes involved in 
moving goods across borders. Together, these words explain the barriers for intra-
African trade and highlight the need for reforms promoted by the AfCFTA. 
4 
… it represents 1.3 billion people and a collective GDP of US$3 trillion across 
a geographic area as vast as the United States, China, India, and much of 
Western Europe put together. (Para. 2) 
Note: This sentence compares the size of the African continent to the size of the US, 
China, India, and Western European countries together. This helps the readers to 
understand how big the continent is and how important its sustainable growth would 
be. 
5 
The World Bank estimates that the AfCFTA could increase the continent’s 
real income by up to 9 percent and lift 50 million people out of extreme 
poverty by 2035 if all policies under consideration are adopted. (Para. 4) 
Note: The phrase “real income” is a term in finance and economics referring to the 
money earned by a person, company, government, etc. over a particular period of time 
that is calculated by taking into account the effect of inflation. The term “real income” 
reflects the purchasing power of the income. For example, we can say “Even though 
she got a pay raise, her real income fell because prices rose faster.” On the contrary, 
“nominal income” is the raw amount of money earned before any adjustments for 
inflation or changes in the cost of living. 
6 
However, turning the aspiration into reality is a marathon, not a sprint, and 
Africa still faces myriad challenges. (Para. 6) 
Note: The two opposite words “marathon” and “sprint” are used metaphorically to 
show the nature of the effort required to turn aspirations into reality. “Marathon” 
implies a long-term, sustained effort, while “not a sprint” emphasizes that it cannot be 
校园资料仓

23 
 
 
achieved quickly or easily, requiring patience and resilience.  
The expression “… is a marathon, not a sprint” is a common metaphor used to 
emphasize the need for endurance, patience, and sustained effort. For example: 
Building a successful business is a marathon, not a sprint. 
Life is a marathon, not a sprint. 
7 
The wealthier African nations, who are likely to be the first to benefit from 
freer trade, should take responsibility and catalyze opportunities for the 
economically weaker states. (Para. 7) 
catalyze: vt. make sth. start happening or start being successful 催动…发生（或成
功） 
His inspiring speech catalyzed a wave of enthusiasm among the team, leading to a 
successful project launch. 
8 
That, in turn, will create demand for components and raw materials from 
other African countries such as Zambia, a major copper producer, or the 
Democratic Republic of the Congo, where cobalt for lithium batteries could 
be mined and processed. (Para. 7) 
Note: The rise of electric vehicles (EVs) has significantly increased the demand for 
copper and cobalt. Compared to traditional internal combustion engine vehicles, EVs 
use substantially more copper for batteries, motors, and charging infrastructure, and 
cobalt for high-performance lithium-ion batteries. 
9 
 While these reforms are crucial, the heavy lifting is still to come … (Para. 9) 
Meaning: Although these reforms are essential, the real challenge still lies ahead. 
the heavy lifting: most of the work involved in sth.; the most difficult part of a task 
（工作）最主要的方面；（任务）最困难的部分 
The heavy lifting in our project involves coordinating the complex logistics and 
ensuring all stakeholders are aligned. 
 
Translation 
非洲贸易的新天地 
1 
塞拉利昂弗里敦的超市货架上摆满了进口商品，从中国的牙签、荷兰的卫
生纸到法国的糖和瑞士的巧克力，应有尽有。然而，这些商品很多也在邻
国生产，如加纳、摩洛哥、尼日利亚和南非。那么，为什么零售商选择从
遥远的国家采购，而不购买非洲当地生产商的产品呢？答案在于错综复杂
的贸易法规和关税，这些法规和关税往往会使非洲内部的贸易成本高、不
稳定且流程烦琐。 
校园资料仓

24 
 
 
2 
非洲贸易一体化长期困于边境管理不断恶化，运输系统效率低下，以及各
国间法律和政策不一致。2019 年启动的非洲大陆自由贸易区（AfCFTA）点
燃了希望的灯塔。迄今55 个非洲联盟成员国中已有54 个批准该协议，代表
了13 亿人口和总计3 万亿美元的国内生产总值，覆盖的地理面积相当于美
国、中国、印度和大部分西欧的面积总和。 
3 
非洲自贸区旨在建立一个商品和服务的统一市场，实现资本和商务人员的
自由流动。加入非洲自贸区的国家必须承诺2034 年前取消至少97%的商品
的关税。消除关税和非关税壁垒将减少贸易成本，促进非洲内部贸易。统
一市场还将使非洲国家增强自身的工业基础，转变经济结构，提升其在全
球舞台上的地位。 
4 
创建一个单一市场具有巨大的潜力。很多相对未开发的领域充满了机遇，
例如服务贸易——全球增长最快、不断提供新就业岗位的行业，以及电子
商务。世界银行估计，如果所有非洲自贸区拟议中的政策被采纳，到2035
年，非洲自贸区将有助于非洲大陆提高多达9%的实际收入，帮助5,000 万
人摆脱极端贫困。 
5 
自2021 年非洲自贸区正式实施以来，非洲大陆各国的协同努力已经取得了
积极成果。尽管全球经济放缓，非洲内部贸易总值在2023 年仍增长至1,922
亿美元，继2022 年10.9%的显著增长后，又提升了3.2%。 
6 
然而，将愿景转化为现实将是一场马拉松，而非短跑。非洲仍面临许多挑
战。例如，一些国家长期受到冲突的困扰，边境程序可能烦琐耗时。此
外，非洲大陆上使用的超过42 种之多的货币造成交易困难，每年给贸易商
带来约50 亿美元的额外成本。 
7 
非洲各国人均国内生产总值差异极大，从布隆迪的221 美元到毛里求斯的
9,100 美元不等，要融合在一起并非易事。但非洲已经迈出了第一步——其
八个区域经济共同体可以作为构建非洲自贸区的基石。那些更富有的非洲
国家可能最先受益于自由贸易，应当承担责任，为经济较弱的国家创造机
会。以汽车生产为例，非洲的三大汽车制造地——埃及、摩洛哥和南非  —
—将受益于非洲汽车市场开放。这将继而催生对其他非洲国家零部件和原
材料的需求，如 主要产铜国赞比亚，或者可为锂电池开采加工钴矿的刚果
民主共和国。 
8 
此外，为了充分实现非洲自贸区的潜力，还需要进行配套改革。非洲各国
需要升级物流，扫清数字商务障碍，并改善营商环境，如提高监管透明度
等。 
校园资料仓

25 
 
 
9 
尽管这些改革至关重要，但最艰巨的任务还在后面——协调各国关于知识
产权、投资保护、电子商务和竞争政策等领域的国内法规。这对于实现预
期的就业、收入和外商直接投资的增长至关重要。更重要的是，非洲自贸
区必须得到非洲大小企业的支持，正是这些企业将利用协议所提供的机
会，推动就业和经济增长。 
10 建立一个自贸区并非易事，其他大洲已有先例。但非洲的商业、政府和民
间各界领导人已展示出推动这一项目的决心。这个全球最大的自贸区向国
际投资者发出了强烈信号，表明非洲正开放市场，实施统一的贸易和投资
规则。非洲大陆自由贸易区协议有望成为非洲经济发展的游戏规则改变
者，并为非洲的未来点燃希望。

Live interview 
Teaching suggestions 
One objective of this activity is to deepen Ss’ understanding of the free trade 
efforts, develop their ability to use the English language through turns of questions and 
answers, and sharpen their communicative skills to participate in dialogues. 
⚫ 
T briefly introduces the scenario, purpose, and roles for the activity, and divides 
the class into three groups. 
⚫ 
T assigns a role to each group randomly, and guides each group to prepare based 
on their assigned roles:  
➢ Interviewer: Develop key questions, anticipate possible answers, and plan 
strategies to facilitate discussion. 
➢ Chinese representative: Prepare answers showcasing China’s free trade 
practices, outcomes, and collaboration opportunities with Africa. 
➢ African representative: Prepare answers showcasing Africa’s free trade 
practices, outcomes, and collaboration opportunities with China. 
⚫ 
(Optional) T rearranges Ss into groups of three with each member from different 
roles, and holds parallel live interviews in the same room. Maximum participation 
can be achieved with every student playing a role in a group.  
⚫ 
T invites one member of the interviewer team to host the interview. Several 
校园资料仓

29 
 
 
members of the Chinese and African representatives are invited to the stage for a 
live interview show. The other Ss take part as the audience who might be invited 
by the interviewer to ask questions. 
⚫ 
The interviewer leads the session, asks questions, and facilitates discussion. 
Representatives respond to the questions and can ask each other questions. 
⚫ 
T asks Ss to write a reflection note, summarizing the key messages covered in the 
interview, and suggesting improvements for future interviews. 
⚫ 
T provides constructive feedback, focusing on intercultural communication skills 
and the depth and relevance of the discussion content. 
 
Sample answers 
Interviewer: Thank you all for joining this discussion today! To start, let’s talk about 
free trade in China and Africa. What are the similarities and differences in the free 
trade practices in China and Africa, and how do they contribute to economic growth? 
Chinese youth representative: Both regions focus on reducing trade barriers and 
fostering economic growth. For example, China’s Hainan Free Trade Port implements 
zero tariffs and streamlined customs procedures, while Africa’s AfCFTA eliminates 
tariffs and harmonizes regulations to boost intra-African trade. However, the scale is 
different – China’s initiatives focus on specific regions like Hainan, whereas AfCFTA 
involves 54 countries, making coordination more complex. Additionally, China’s 
infrastructure is well-established, while Africa is still building the necessary systems 
to support free trade. 
African youth representative: I agree. Both aim to promote regional integration and 
connectivity, recognizing that these are key to unlocking economic potential. 
However, Africa’s diversity in legal systems and economic development stages 
presents unique challenges. Despite this, the AfCFTA is fostering cooperation among 
nations, while China’s targeted strategies provide valuable lessons for tailoring 
policies to local conditions. 
Interviewer: Thank you! Now, let’s discuss the outcomes and future collaboration. 
What have been the key achievements, and how can China and Africa work together 
to advance free trade? 
Chinese youth representative: Including the Hainan FTP, China has established 21 
free trade zones across the country, playing a pivotal role in enhancing foreign trade, 
driving economic structural transformation and upgrading, and deepening integration 
between domestic and global markets. One collaboration opportunity is infrastructure 
development – China’s expertise in building transportation networks can help Africa 
strengthen trade corridors under the AfCFTA framework. 
African youth representative: Africa’s AfCFTA is increasing intra-African trade 
校园资料仓

30 
 
 
and bringing nations closer together. Collaboration in agriculture is another 
opportunity – Africa’s resources combined with China’s advanced farming 
technologies can enhance productivity and create value-added products for global 
markets. 
Interviewer: Thank you both for your insights! It’s clear that free trade opens up 
huge opportunities for collaboration between China and Africa, paving the way for 
shared growth and prosperity. 
 
Sample question bank: 
1 
What did China do to promote free trade? 
2 
What has China achieved in promoting free trade? 
3 
What lessons and experiences can China offer to Africa? 
4 
What can China learn from Africa’s experience in the building of a united market 
across the continent? 
5 
Why do African countries want a single market across the continent? 
6 
Why is free trade between African countries important? 
7 
What has the AfCFTA achieved so far? 
8 
What lessons and experiences can Africa offer to China? 
9 
What can Africa learn from China’s reform and opening up? 
10 What similarities do China and African countries share in their efforts for growth? 
11 What are the differences between China and Africa in their exploration of the right 
path of development? 
12 What are some of the biggest challenges Africa faces in implementing free trade 
agreements, and how can China offer support in addressing these challenges? 
13 What are some potential areas for future collaboration between China and Africa? 
14 How can young people from China and Africa contribute to strengthening the ties 
between the two regions? 
 
 
校园资料仓

31 
 
 
Communicating with the world  
Additional notes  
The key objective of an expository speech is to inform the audience about the 
chosen topic. To ensure good understanding from the audience, the speaker should first 
select and organize the target messages based on the audience’s background knowledge 
about the issue. T can guide Ss to review the video and the two texts in the unit and 
discuss how to simplify and adapt information about China’s ongoing reform and 
opening up to suit the audience’s knowledge level. Another important consideration is 
how to present the selected messages. T can guide Ss to use situation-based narratives 
in their talk to make their speech more engaging. An engaging expository speech 
requires the effective use of public speaking skills. T can guide Ss to refine their 
speeches by incorporating techniques they have learned, such as crafting a good hook, 
asking interactive questions, presenting a specific scenario, and delivering a powerful 
ending.  
For evaluation of the speech, consider the following perspectives: 
⚫ 
Content: Clearly explain China’s reform and opening up and the Hainan FTP with 
accurate facts, engaging cases, and insightful comments. 
⚫ 
Structure: Develop a complete speech with good logical flow, transitioning 
smoothly from greeting the audience and a hook to a brief overview, then to the 
Hainan FTP and a natural conclusion. 
⚫ 
Audience connection: Engage the audience with a strong hook and interactive 
questions, relating to international audiences’ interests. 
⚫ 
Language: Use concise and vivid language appropriate for a speech. 
⚫ 
Cultural sensitivity: Balances pride with inclusivity, addressing stereotyping and 
misconceptions. 
*Remind Ss to access the video micro-lecture in the digital textbook to learn more 
about the skill on Ucampus. 
 
 
Sample answers  
Distinguished guests and fellow students,  
Good morning!  
Imagine this: You are walking along a fine sandy beach, with crystal-clear waves 
gently massaging your feet. How do you feel? Relaxed, comfortable? Can you associate 
this place with the forefront of China’s reform and opening up? This tropical paradise 
is now a testing ground for the future of open economies.  
校园资料仓

32 
 
 
Hainan’s transformation mirrors China’s 40-year journey from poverty to 
prosperity. In 1978, China opened its door with a bold experiment: setting up special 
economic zones like Shenzhen. Under the new policies, foreign investment, 
technology, and trade flooded in. In 2001, China joined the WTO with all kinds of 
goods produced and shipped to almost every corner of the world. 
The results? Small villages like Shenzhen are now modern cities with skyscrapers 
rising into the clouds; China has grown into the world’s second-largest economy, and 
the whole world benefits from China’s products. But the real miracle is that over 800 
million Chinese people were lifted out of poverty – that’s like lifting every person in 
Europe twice. These achievements show the power of reform: not just growth, but 
transformation. 
Now, meet Hainan 2.0! In 2018, the Hainan Island became the Free Trade Port 
with policies bolder than Shenzhen’s early days – tax incentives, simplified procedures, 
tariff exemptions, visa-free entry – all these show the highest level of openness in 
today’s world. By 2035, Hainan envisions becoming an international trade hub 
comparable to Singapore and Dubai. During a short tour in Hainan, we could explore 
its emerging potential: Will Haikou’s tech parks or Sanya’s cruise ports lead the way in 
positioning Hainan as the “next Shenzhen”? 
Many of you come from countries like Kenya, Nigeria, or Egypt – places that are 
actively building your own trade blocs like the AfCFTA. During this study tour, let’s 
explore Hainan’s policies – examining the successes and the challenges – and discuss 
how similar strategies could be adopted for cities like Lagos, Nairobi, or Dhaka. What 
might work? What might need rethinking? China’s development experience offers one 
perspective in a broader global conversation. While our reforms have significantly 
transformed our economy, your nations have the potential to innovate and chart unique 
paths forward.  
Hainan proves that even paradise can innovate. As future leaders, you’re not just 
witnesses to China’s story – you’re authors of your own. So let’s dive in, question 
openly, and connect deeply. The next chapter of global growth starts here, with us. 
Thank you – and let the journey begin! 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
校园资料仓

## U4（34 页）

1 
 
U4 The rule of law 
 
Unit overview 
The rule of law is a fundamental principle ensuring the equal application of the 
law, the protection of the rights of citizens, and the preservation of social order. This 
unit explores the evolution of legal systems both in China and globally, with a focus on 
grassroots legal participation, public legal education, and the practical application of 
laws.  
The Understanding China section examines China’s local legal systems, from the 
Miao people’s traditions to modern innovations such as online and circuit courts, which 
enhance legal accessibility in rural areas. It also explores the ongoing development of 
the rule of law, focusing on key achievements like the Hongqiao legislative contact 
station and the Civil Code.  
The Exploring the world section provides a comparative perspective, showcasing 
the UK jury system and its emphasis on the role of citizens in the judicial process. This 
contrast helps Ss appreciate the varied approaches to legal participation across cultures. 
The Communicating with the world section challenges Ss to create a publicity 
video that highlights China’s legal advancements and their positive impact on society 
and individuals. This activity deepens Ss’ understanding of the intersection between 
legal systems and social progress, fostering a global perspective on the role of law in 
safeguarding national development and prosperity. 
 
思政元素清单 
Understanding China — Viewing 
 
学法懂法是守法用法的前提。 
 
要努力让人民群众在每一个司法案件中感受到公平正义。 
 
依法治国必须重视传统文化的作用，推进法治建设的同时要继承和弘扬中
华优秀传统文化。 
Understanding China — Reading 
 
全面依法治国最广泛、最深厚的基础是人民，必须坚持为了人民、依靠人
民。 
 
公民直接参与立法过程，体现了“人民当家作主”的理念。 
 
加快完善中国特色社会主义法律体系，以良法促进发展、保障善治。

2 
 
Exploring the world — Reading  
 
司法民主化是普遍追求，公民参与司法正是这一理念的重要实践。 
 
法治公信力的核心在于司法透明度和程序正义。 
 
公民的法律意识与和法治信仰是现代法治建设的社会基础。 
 
单元开篇 
Quote  
全面依法治国是国家治理的一场深刻革命，必须坚持厉行法治，推进科学立
法、严格执法、公正司法、全民守法。 
——2017 年10 月18 日，习近平在中国共产党第十九次全国代表大会上的报告 
 
Key facts about China 
The key facts in this unit reflect China’s multifaceted approach to creating a robust 
legal system. They highlight efforts to ensure participation in the lawmaking process, 
maintain public security, and promote legal education. Together, these data suggest a 
legal system that is not only focused on governance but also on shaping societal values. 
T can guide Ss to access the digital textbook for the latest information and data. Here’s 
how the selected key facts connect to the quote: 
Key fact 1: This fact reflects a strong sense of law and order in China. This could be 
attributed to a combination of factors, including effective law enforcement, stringent 
security measures, and a generally high level of public compliance with laws. The high 
public security index underscores China’s reputation for being a safe and stable country. 
Key fact 2: This fact reflects a maturing legal system that prioritizes quality over mere 
quantity. These laws are well-crafted and aligned with high-quality development goals, 
supporting governance, innovation, and social stability. This evolution reinforces the 
rule of law as a core foundation of China’s modern development. 
Key fact 3: With nearly 92 percent of enacted / amended laws (156 out of 170) 
incorporating grassroots feedback since 2015, this fact reflects a bottom-up approach 
to lawmaking, which enhances the legitimacy and inclusiveness of China’s legal 
system.  
Key fact 4:  This fact demonstrates a strategic push to improve legal literacy across 
China, aligning with the government’s commitment to fostering a culture of rule of law 
across all sections of society.  
*T can guide Ss to access the digital textbook for the latest information and data 
on Ucampus.

3 
 
Teaching suggestions 
Step 1: Begin by asking Ss to reflect on the meaning of the “rule of law” and its function 
in different societies. Use the quote to stimulate discussion. T can ask follow-up 
questions like “Why is ‘advancing law-based governance’ called a ‘profound 
revolution’?” T can also show the Nanjing Constitution Park image and ask questions 
like: How do public spaces like this promote the rule of law? 
Step 2: Divide Ss into small groups to review and discuss the significance of key facts 
about China’s legal system. T can link Ss’ findings to “sound lawmaking / strict 
enforcement” in the quote. 
Step 3: Introduce Scenario and ask Ss to brainstorm legal practices that impact their 
daily life in China.  
Step 4: Present the unit’s learning objectives and explain how this unit enhances Ss’ 
understanding of China’s legal system. Highlight how the unit fosters cross-cultural 
understanding by comparing legal systems in China with those in other parts of the 
world, helping Ss develop a more comprehensive perspective on modern legal 
practices.

4 
 
Understanding China 
The video and reading materials in this section offer a rich exploration of China’s 
evolving legal system, focusing on grassroots participation and innovative legal 
practices. The video highlights localized efforts in Rongshui, Guangxi, to make legal 
processes more accessible through initiatives like mock trials, remote mediation, and 
circuit courts in rural areas. These efforts exemplify the commitment to ensuring equal 
access to justice for all citizens.  Similarly, the reading material discusses grassroots 
involvement in lawmaking through national-level legislative contact stations and the 
Civil Code’s response to society’s evolving needs. 
Both materials showcase China’s commitment to a people-centered legal system. 
The teaching focus for this unit should center on the concepts of legal accessibility and 
public participation, while the difficulty lies in enabling Ss to critically assess how these 
reforms are aligned with the broader principles of justice and legal evolution. The 
challenge for Ss is to understand how China integrates modern legal practices with 
traditional values and addresses contemporary societal issues.  
 
Viewing  
Before viewing 
Teaching suggestions  
The four definitions of law in Activity 1 (P81) of the Students’ Book demonstrate 
different perspectives drawn from philosophy, sociology, legal theory, and modern 
practice. They are intended to help Ss understand that law is not a single concept, but 
one that varies depending on its purpose, function, or historical background. T can use 
these definitions to guide classroom discussion, enabling Ss to deepen understanding 
and relate abstract ideas to real-world examples. 
A. Law is a rule of conduct, a contract, an ideal of reason. 
This definition reflects classical natural law theory, influenced by thinkers like 
Aristotle and Thomas Aquinas. Law is not merely a set of rules, but a guide for behavior, 
a moral contract among people, and a rational ideal grounded in justice.  
T can encourage Ss to see law as more than punishment – a system rooted in ethics 
and reason. 
B. Law is a form of social control. 
Attributed to Plato, this view emphasizes law’s role in maintaining social order. 
Law regulates behavior, prevents conflict, and reinforces societal norms. 
T can use real-life examples (e.g., traffic rules, school policies) to help Ss 
understand how law shapes daily life. 
C. Law is a rule of civil conduct prescribed by the supreme power in a state, 
commanding what is right and prohibiting what is wrong. 
This legal positivist definition is associated with William Blackstone. It defines

5 
 
law as authoritative commands from the state, intended to regulate civil behavior and 
enforce moral boundaries. 
T can introduce Ss to the formal structure of law – where authority, power, and 
enforcement are central. 
D. Law consists of enforceable rules governing relationships among individuals 
and between individuals and their society. 
This modern, functional definition appears in many legal textbooks. Law is seen 
as a system of enforceable rules that define rights, regulate relationships, and balance 
private and public interests. 
T can use familiar examples like contracts, property rights, or consumer laws to 
show how law operates in daily life. 
 
Sample answers 
1  
I personally think D makes the most sense. This definition captures the essence of 
law in a more comprehensive and practical way, highlighting not only its role in 
governing interactions between people but also its enforceability, which is key to its 
effectiveness.  
My own definition of law would be: Law is a system of rules established by 
authorities to regulate behavior, resolve disputes, and protect rights within a 
society. This definition encompasses the functions of law – guiding behavior, resolving 
conflicts, and protecting rights – while also implying that law is grounded in legitimate 
authority and informed by societal needs and values, which are crucial for its 
legitimacy. 
2   
I think B and C are the most effective ways to spread legal knowledge. 
⚫ 
B is effective because it provides a direct and personal way for people to learn from 
experienced professionals who can explain complex legal concepts clearly. It also 
allows for interaction and discussion, which helps deepen understanding. 
⚫ 
C is effective because media and entertainment products like TV programs and 
dramas can reach a wider audience and make legal concepts more relatable. 
Through storytelling, people can see how laws are applied in real-life situations, 
making it easier to understand their relevance and importance. 
 
Alternatively, I think A and D are the most effective ways to spread legal 
knowledge. 
⚫ 
A is effective because it reaches a broad audience in everyday settings. Slogans are 
short and memorable, making key legal concepts easy to understand and

6 
 
remember. Brochures can provide more detailed information in a simple, visually 
engaging format. This method is especially useful in communities with limited 
access to online or formal legal education. However, it works best when combined 
with other interactive or media-based approaches. 
⚫ 
D is a fun and engaging way to promote legal awareness, especially among 
students and young people. They encourage participants to actively study laws and 
understand their rights and responsibilities. When held in schools or communities, 
they can create a positive learning atmosphere and increase interest in legal topics.  
 
In addition to traditional methods, there are several innovative ways to popularize 
legal knowledge:  
⚫ 
Social media campaigns for younger audiences 
⚫ 
Community legal clinics / street law programs offering free legal advice directly 
to the public  
⚫ 
School curriculum integration for early awareness of rights and responsibilities  
⚫ 
Online platforms / mobile apps offer convenient access to legal information 
⚫ 
National campaigns like Constitution Day 
⚫ 
Interactive tools (quizzes / mock trials)   
⚫ 
These diverse approaches help make legal knowledge more accessible, practical, 
and relevant to everyday life. 
Scripts 
[Anchor]: I found these pictures in the materials. It looks like they are just ordinary 
stones. There is no text. A long time ago, in order to establish township regulations and 
resolve disputes, the Miao people buried them in places where land disputes existed, in 
this way to endow the authority of the regulations, similar to the Hammurabi of ancient 
Babylon. Hundreds of years have passed, but what does it look like now? What do they 
rely on to resolve problems now? 
I came to Rongshui, Guangxi, where the Miao people live. I hope to find my answer 
here. 
Scene 1 
[Student]: Please be quiet. The court is ready for session. Public prosecutor, legal 
representatives, and defense attorney are invited to appear in court. 
[Anchor]: The Rongshui Miao Autonomous County People’s Juvenile Criminal Moot 
Court is now in session. Defendant, his representatives, and his attorney…. 
I was riveted. It felt like I was in a real trial. The students were so professional. It (the 
moot court) turned the law, which can be very abstract, into something that’s easy to 
follow and easy to understand. This is just one of the examples of how we can

7 
 
popularize the law more and more to all the members of society by actually making it 
accessible. 
Scene 2 
[Staff]: Today I will show you around the Rongshui Court. Please follow me. This is 
our Shared Remote Mediating Room. This is Wangdong People’s Court. 
[Anchor]: Good morning, your honor. 
[Judge]: Good morning! 
[Anchor]: Engaging in a lawsuit is a complicated process. But the new lawsuit service 
reduces so much of the stress related to it. You can still get all the legal benefits you 
deserve without having to spend so much time and money. It improves the efficiency 
of law enforcement for everyone. 
So, have you used the new lawsuit service yet? And what do you think about it? 
[Interviewee A]: I think this online court service is of great help to the people. I don’t 
even need to go out to keep abreast of my case. 
[Interviewee B]: We are getting better now, as we are protected by law. 
[Interviewee C]: Now we know more about the law. 
[Anchor]: And how has the new lawsuit service changed your life？ 
[Interviewee D]: Now in our countryside, the service is the same as in the city, very 
convenient and efficient. Just three words: It is great! 
Scene 3 
[Anchor]: I wonder what the judge is doing with that emblem and where he’s going 
with it. Excuse me, sir! Where are you going？ 
[Judge]: We are going to the countryside to handle cases. 
[Anchor]: I would love to see that. Can I come with you？ 
[Judge]: OK! 
[Anchor]: Why do you take the emblem with you when you handle cases in the 
countryside？ 
[Judge]: The national emblem is what we must carry on the circuit. Because it 
represents authority and justice. When we handle cases in the circuit trial mode, usually, 
we can only set up simple courts. But we still need to honor the authority of the law. 
So, no matter where we go, we will carry the national emblem. 
[Anchor]: It looks like it’s a lot of effort to come all the way here, and far distances to 
travel. Why do you set up courts like this？ 
[Judge]: We took the initiative to bring the court to the people’s homes. The first reason 
is for the convenience of the local residents, to help them save time and money. The 
second reason is that, in our jurisdiction, people’s knowledge of the law is relatively

8 
 
poor. When we set up the court at their homes to help them out, we are also enhancing 
their legal knowledge to some extent and improving their legal literacy. That’s why we 
took the initiative of setting up courts in people’s homes. 
[Anchor]: So, what kind of cases do you usually handle? 
[Judge]: Every year there are divorce disputes, private lending disputes, and disputes 
among neighbors. Cases in these three categories are about the same numbers. 
[Anchor]: And how many cases are roughly taken here? 
[Judge]: Our circuit court handles about 400 cases a year. 
[Anchor]: The (circuit court) judges certainly are very different to what I’m used to. 
They kind of make me think of legal superheroes carrying their emblem through the 
rain and the misty mountains to get to the people and serve them justice who otherwise 
probably wouldn’t be able to access it.  
Our trip here has been very interesting. We got to participate in a mock trial. We saw 
all the ways that technology is making it easier to access the law. And we even got to 
observe a circuit court in a small village. Nowadays, there are more and more ways of 
popularizing the law and enforcing it efficiently. The law is not just some abstract thing 
that’s only relevant to legal professionals. It’s all around us. It influences all of us. The 
improvement of the legal system continues to enhance people’s sense of satisfaction. 
And I believe better access to law and raising awareness of the law will create a better 
and happier society. 
 
Notes 
Hammurabi of ancient Babylon refers to the Code of Hammurabi, issued by 
Hammurabi, a king of ancient Babylon, during the 18th century BC. It is also known 
as the “Stele Code,” due to its inscription on a black stone. It was once considered the 
oldest known set of written laws in human history, though older, shorter law collections 
have since been found. It contains 282 laws addressing civil, criminal, and commercial 
matters.     
 
Language points 
1 
The court is ready for session. Public Prosecutor, Legal Representatives, and 
Defense Attorney are invited to appear in court. (Scene 1) 
session: n. [C] a formal meeting or group of meetings, esp. of a law court or parliament
（法庭的）开庭 
The judge announced that the court was ready for session after a brief delay. 
appear: vi. go to a law court or other official meeting to give information, answer 
questions, etc. 出庭；出席 
Witnesses were summoned to appear in court to provide testimony.

9 
 
Note: “Appear in court” is a formal phrase typically used in legal settings. It implies 
that someone is physically present in court for a legal proceeding but can also refer to 
participating virtually in some modern court systems (e.g., video conference hearings). 
2 
This is just one of the examples of how we can popularize the law more and 
more to all the members of society by actually making it accessible. (Scene 1) 
popularize: vt. make a difficult subject or idea able to be easily understood by ordinary 
people who have no special knowledge about it 使普及；使通俗化 
We need to popularize sustainable practices in every community to ensure 
environmental protection. 
accessible: a. easy to obtain or use 可使用的；可及的 
Nowadays, AI apps are accessible to a large number of people around the world, 
though some may still face barriers. 
3 
I don’t even need to go out to keep abreast of my case. (Scene 2) 
keep / stay abreast of sth.: make sure that you know all the most recent facts or 
information about a particular subject or situation 了解某事的最新情况 
Lawyers must keep abreast of changing regulations to provide the best counsel. 
Note: This phrase is often used in professional or formal settings to discuss the 
importance of remaining informed about something. It suggests a proactive approach 
to keeping up-to-date with the developments in one’s field, often in law, business, and 
academia. It can be used in less formal contexts too, though with a slightly more 
elevated tone. 
4 
That’s why we took the initiative of setting up courts in people’s homes.  
(Scene 3) 
take the initiative: if you take the initiative in a situation, you are the first person to 
act, and are therefore able to control the situation 主动；带头 
The company took the initiative to launch a new environmental campaign. 
Note: This phrase conveys a sense of leadership, independence, and proactive behavior. 
In both professional and social contexts, it suggests someone who is ready to act and 
take charge of a situation, often without waiting for others to act first. 
 
Viewing and understanding  
Reference answers  
2  
1   T 
2   F  
[Corrected: The Rongshui Miao Autonomous County People’s Juvenile Criminal 
Moot Court is a mock court designed for educational purposes.]

10 
 
3   T 
5 F  
[Corrected: The new lawsuit service allows people to manage their cases online, 
reducing the need for frequent travel to court.] 
5   T 
6   T 
6 F  
[Corrected: Circuit courts in the countryside handle various types of disputes, 
including divorce, private lending, and neighbor disputes.] 
7 F  
[Corrected: The video suggests that the law is relevant to everyone, not just legal 
professionals, and it influences all aspects of life.] 
 
Intercultural communication skill 
Teaching suggestions 
This activity aims to develop Ss’ skills in crafting engaging, curiosity-driven, and 
guiding questions for creating an impactful publicity video on China’s rule of law and 
its positive influence on people’s lives. 
⚫ 
Introduce the three types of questions: 
➢ Curiosity-driven questions should spark the audience’s interest by addressing 
themes that resonate with their experiences or curiosity.  
➢ Relatable questions should invite personal responses or shared experiences, 
helping the audience connect with the subject matter.  
➢ Guiding questions ensure the narrative remains focused and progresses 
logically.  
It’s important that the questions are balanced, allowing flexibility while still 
maintaining clarity. 
⚫ 
Give a brief overview of the project context:

Sample answers 
3

Curiosity-driven questions:

Reading 
Text: Law on our doorstep 
Background information 
The rule of law is essential for building a modern socialist country, ensuring the 
protection of rights and social stability. The rule of law is viewed not only as a 
mechanism for governance but as a tool for fostering fairness, justice, and 
accountability in society. The rule of law in China goes beyond merely enacting laws; 
it involves comprehensive reforms in governance, legal education, judicial 
independence, and public participation in the legal process. This is exemplified in the 
text by grassroots involvement through legislative contact stations and the 
modernization of laws, such as the Civil Code.  
In line with the modernization of its legal system, China has introduced 
technological advancements to improve judicial efficiency and accessibility. These 
innovations include online courts, AI-powered legal tools, and digital platforms for 
public participation. Since 2017, China has established online courts to make legal 
proceedings more accessible, especially for citizens in remote areas. This initiative has 
greatly reduced the cost and time associated with legal processes, making justice more 
efficient. Additionally, China has invested in AI tools to assist with legal research, case 
analysis, and even the drafting of legal documents.  
The overall aim of these legal advancements is to enhance the daily lives of 
Chinese people by providing greater access to justice, ensuring social stability, and 
addressing emerging societal challenges.

12 
 
Notes 
Laws, regulations, acts, guidance, and policies are terms that are often used 
interchangeably in casual contexts, but they have distinct legal meanings. Acts are 
primary legislation enacted by national or regional legislatures, establishing 
fundamental legal frameworks. Laws encompass both acts and subsidiary rules, 
enforceable by courts, enforceable through judicial mechanisms.  Regulations are 
detailed implementation rules issued by executive bodies under legislative delegation. 
Guidance offers recommendations on how to comply with regulations, but it is not 
enforceable. Policies, set by organizations or governments, outline expected behaviors 
and goals, often supported by laws. The key differences between the terms include the 
scope, enforceability, and origin, with laws and acts being legally binding, unlike 
guidance and policies. 
*T can guide Ss to study the multimodal annotations in the digital textbook on 
Ucampus.  
 
 
Language points  
1 
In the sweltering summer heat of Shanghai, a small room in the Gubei Civic 
Center buzzes with heated discussion. (Para. 1) 
buzz: vi. (~ with) be full of excitement, activity, etc. 充满兴奋（或活动等） 
The office buzzes with excitement ahead of the big presentation. 
Note: “Buzz with” is a figurative phrase that creates a vivid image of a place alive with 
energy or movement. It’s particularly useful in describing places or situations where 
there is a lot of conversation, action, or emotional energy. In this context, the phrase 
suggests a high-energy environment with intense discussion or activity. 
2 
The fervor of the debate mirrors the oppressive heat outside as participants 
grapple with the pressing question … (Para. 1)  
mirror: vt. if one thing mirrors another, it is very similar to it and may seem to copy or 
represent it 与…相似 
His calm demeanor mirrors that of his father, who is always composed in difficult 
situations. 
Note: “Mirror” is often used metaphorically to suggest that one thing reflects or closely 
corresponds to another, indicating a parallel or similarity. It is commonly used in both 
literal (reflections in a mirror) and figurative (ideas, behaviors, or conditions) contexts. 
grapple with: try hard to deal with or understand sth. difficult 尽力解决，设法理解
（难题） 
She grapples with the decision of whether to move abroad or stay in her hometown.

13 
 
3 
Every citizen can roll up their sleeves and engage in the process of making or 
amending the law, truly taking ownership.  (Para. 2) 
roll up one’s sleeves: start doing a job, even though it is difficult 卷起袖子（准备苦
干） 
If we want to improve the community, we need to roll up our sleeves and start working 
together. 
Note: This phrase is often used in informal settings to suggest someone is ready to work 
or face challenges. It invokes a sense of readiness and a hands-on approach. This phrase 
is especially effective when encouraging someone to take personal initiative. Similar 
expressions include “get involved” and “take action.” 
4 
I thought the legislative contact station would be tucked away in a grand, 
secluded building or an office tower. (Para. 2) 
be tucked away:  be located in a quiet place,  where not many people go 坐落在，位
于（僻静的地方）  
The library was tucked away in a corner of the campus.  
Note: The key expressions “tucked away,” “grand,” and “secluded” highlight the 
connotations of spaces in different contexts. These words show how legislative systems 
are viewed in different cultures. The idea of a “secluded” or “grand” building contrasts 
sharply with the transparency and public engagement seen in the legislative contact 
stations.    
5 
In addition to involving ordinary citizens, China has also made great strides 
in enacting new laws to deal with novel issues arising from the rapid 
development of society. (Para. 4) 
make great strides: make significant progress or advances 取得巨大进步 
The country has made great strides in improving healthcare over the last decade. 
Note: This phrase emphasizes progress and is often used in formal or academic contexts 
to highlight positive developments. It’s a stronger expression than simply saying “made 
progress,” implying a considerable or notable improvement. 
enact: v. make a proposal into a law 制定，通过，颁布（法令） 
The government is focused on enacting stricter laws to protect the environment. 
Note: This word is used primarily in legal and legislative contexts; “enacting” refers to 
the formal process of passing laws. It carries a sense of official action and authority. 
Note: This transitional sentence shifts the focus from grassroots legislative 
participation to China’s broader legislative achievements, especially the Civil Code. It 
marks a textual pivot from “who participates in lawmaking” to “what laws are being 
made and why,” showing the dual emphasis on public engagement and modern 
legislative responsiveness in China’s rule of law development.

14 
 
6 
The Civil Code has stepped up to clamp down on such dangers by holding 
property management companies accountable for keeping pedestrians safe. 
(Para. 5) 
clamp down on: take strong action to stop or limit a harmful or unwanted activity 取
缔；严禁；压制 
Universities now clamp down on plagiarism with AI detection tools. 
Note: This phrase is commonly used in legal or regulatory contexts to indicate the 
enforcement of rules or the application of stricter measures. It suggests a determined 
and often forceful effort to control or eliminate a problem. 
hold sb. accountable for sth.: make sb. responsible for what they do and demand a 
satisfactory reason for it 要…负责；要…作出解释 
The company will be held accountable for any environmental damage caused by its 
operations. 
Note: This is commonly used in legal, professional, or ethical discussions to ensure that 
individuals or organizations take responsibility for their actions. It suggests that failure 
to meet obligations will lead to consequences. 
7 
If the French Civil Code symbolized the windmill and watermill era of the 
19th century, and the German Civil Code represented the industrial age, then 
China’s Civil Code is the definitive legal framework for the 21st-century 
Internet and high-tech era. (Para. 6) 
Note: The sentence uses a conditional structure (“If ... then ...”) to compare the Civil 
Codes of France, Germany, and China, linking each to a historical period. This structure 
is often used to present hypothetical scenarios, draw comparisons, or offer contrasts. 
Metaphors like “the windmill and watermill era” and “the industrial age” visually 
reinforce legal evolution, making abstract concepts more tangible. This rhetorical 
device underscores how legal systems adapt to technological and societal change.  
8 
For instance, it includes provisions for the protection of personal data in the 
digital age, a feature not found in many other civil codes. (Para. 6) 
provision: n. [C] a condition in an agreement or law 规定 
The law has provisions that ensure equal pay for equal work. 
Note: In the context of legal discussions, “provisions” are used to describe specific 
clauses within laws or regulations. 
9 
It’s a dynamic framework that adapts to the challenges of the new era by 
protecting rights and maintaining order while staying true to the enduring 
principles of justice and fairness. (Para. 7) 
Meaning: It’s a flexible system that evolves to meet the challenges of the modern world 
by safeguarding rights and maintaining social order, all while staying faithful to the 
timeless values of justice and fairness.

15 
 
Note: In this sentence, the vocabulary and concepts, such as “framework,” “adapts to,” 
“maintaining order,” and “justice”, are also applicable in fields like political science, 
philosophy, and sociology. Ss can use these phrases when discussing changes in laws 
or policies or when debating global issues related to law, technology, or ethics.  
10 As China strides into a new phase, this robust legal system lights the way, 
guiding the nation through uncharted waters, forging the path of the rule of 
law, and offering its people a thriving and safe future. (Para. 7) 
Note: “Light the way” and “uncharted waters” are both metaphorical expressions. 
These metaphors serve to create vivid imagery, allowing readers to conceptualize the 
abstract ideas of change and progress. These types of expressions are useful for adding 
impact and making the text more engaging.  
 
Translation  
法律就在我们身边 
1 
在上海炎热的夏季，古北市民中心的一个小房间内人们正热火朝天地讨论
着。辩论的热度与室外的酷暑交相呼应。参与者们正热烈探讨一个迫切的
问题：失亲儿童应该由谁照顾？这并非一场普通的聚会，而是在虹桥街道
的国家级基层立法联系点举行的讨论。普通市民在这里汇聚一堂，共同为
国家法律的制定建言献策。 
2 
类似的场景在虹桥立法联系点屡见不鲜。参与讨论的一位市民自豪地说道：
“法律可能看起来很遥远，但实际上，它就在我们身边。每一位公民都可
以撸起袖子，参与到法律的制定或修订过程中，真正当家作主。”立法联系
点就位于街头，欢迎市民自由参与。一位来自美国的访问代表对这种开放
性表示惊讶，评论道：“我本以为立法联系点应该隐藏在某个宏伟的、隐蔽
的建筑或写字楼中，但它竟然就在街边。如果我没亲眼看到，真是不敢相
信。” 
3 
虹桥立法联系点是当前45 个国家级立法联系点的一个缩影，它帮助表达民
众的关切，将他们的愿望编织进充满活力的民主立法画卷中。这些联系点
与日益壮大的地方联系点网络共同作用，像溪流汇入浩瀚大河，借助生动
的座谈会、坦诚的访谈和深入的实地调研，收集民意涓流，并使其奔涌向
前。它们已成为重要的信息源，确保普通民众的声音不仅被听到，而且能
够迅速体现在国家法律中，推动中国法律向更广泛的公众参与发展。 
4 
除了让普通市民参与立法，中国还在制定新法律方面取得了显著进展，以
应对社会快速发展带来的新问题。这些法律涉及国家安全、公共卫生、生
态环境等关键问题，为信息时代设定了标准，并维护了伦理价值。其中，
最为突出的法律之一便是《民法典》——中国首部正式命名为“法典”的
法律。

16 
 
5 
《中华人民共和国民法典》于2021 年颁布，是对现行民事法律、法规及司
法解释的全面汇编。它常被誉为“社会生活百科全书”，为当今许多紧迫问
题提供了明确的解决方案。比如，公共交通上的不文明行为，如乘客占用
不属于自己的座位，《民法典》明确将这种行为定为非法，从而维护了公共
交通秩序与安全。你是否曾担心走在街上时，可能被从楼上掉落的物体砸
伤？《民法典》通过要求物业管理公司为行人的安全负责，加大力度遏制
此类风险。那么关于七十年住宅建设用地使用权到期后的担忧呢？《民法
典》确定了自动续期的规定。通过解决这些社会热点问题，《民法典》全面
保障公民的权利，已成为我们日常生活中不可或缺的重要组成部分。 
6 
《民法典》不仅反应民众的诉求，还响应着时代节奏的变化。如果法国
《民法典》象征着19 世纪风车和水磨时代，德国《民法典》代表着工业时
代，那么中国的《民法典》则是21 世纪互联网和高科技时代的标志性法律
框架。它应对信息时代技术爆炸带来的挑战，例如，它包括了数字时代对
个人数据保护的条款，这在许多其他民法典中并没有体现。同时，它还回
应了环境保护和生态保护等问题，倡导建设一个人与自然和谐共生的现代
社会。 
7 
中国的法律体系不仅仅是一套法律条文，它是一股活跃且不断发展的力量，
与国家的脉搏同频共振。从城市的繁忙街道到乡村的宁静角落，这一体系
赋予公民积极参与制定治理其生活的法律的权利。这是一个动态的框架，
它通过保护权利和维持秩序应对新时代的挑战，同时坚持正义和公平这一
永恒原则。随着中国迈入新阶段，这一强大的法律体系为国家照亮前行的
道路，引领着中国穿越未知的领域，开辟法治之路，为人民提供一个繁荣
且安全的未来。 
 
Reading and understanding  
Reference answers 
1

2) public involvement
